    10→use jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;
    11→use jj_lib::repo::{ReadonlyRepo, Repo};
    12→use jj_lib::workspace::Workspace;
    13→use pollster::FutureExt;
    14→
    15→use crate::common;
    16→
    17→#[derive(Args, Debug)]
    18→pub struct ShowArgs {
    19→    /// Revision (positional)
    20→    #[arg()]
    21→    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,
    22→
    23→    /// Max changed files to display (positional, 0 = all)
    24→    #[arg()]
    25→    pub pos_limit: Option<usize>,
    26→
    27→    /// Revision to show (default: @)
    28→    #[arg(short, long, default_value = "@")]
    29→    pub revision: String,
    30→
    31→    /// Max changed files to display (default: 50, 0 = all)
    32→    #[arg(short = 'n', long, default_value = "50")]
    33→    pub limit: usize,
    34→
    35→    /// Path to jj repo (default: current directory)
    36→    #[arg(short = 'R', long, default_value = ".")]
    37→    pub repo: PathBuf,
    38→}
    39→
    40→pub fn show(args: &ShowArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    41→    // Positional rev overrides -r if -r is still default
    42→    let rev = if let Some(ref pos) = args.pos_rev {
    43→        if args.revision == "@" {
    44→            pos.as_str()
    45→        } else {
    46→            args.revision.as_str()
    47→        }
    48→    } else {
    49→        args.revision.as_str()
    50→    };
    51→
    52→    // Positional limit overrides -n if -n is still default
    53→    let file_limit = if let Some(pos_n) = args.pos_limit {
    54→        pos_n
    55→    } else {
    56→        args.limit
    57→    };
    58→
    59→    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&args.repo)?;
    60→    let commit_ids = common::resolve_revset(&workspace, &repo, rev)?;
    61→
    62→    if commit_ids.is_empty() {
    63→        return Err(format!("no commit found for revision '{rev}'").into());
    64→    }
    65→
    66→    let root_commit_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
    67→    let commit_id = &commit_ids[0];
    68→    if *commit_id == root_commit_id {
    69→        return Err("cannot show root commit".into());
    70→    }
    71→
    72→    let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
    73→
    74→    print_header(&commit, &workspace, &repo)?;
    75→    print_description(&commit);
    76→    print_diff(&commit, &repo, file_limit)?;
    77→
    78→    Ok(())
    79→}
    80→
    81→fn print_header(
    82→    commit: &Commit,
    83→    workspace: &Workspace,
    84→    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
    85→) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    86→    // Ids
    87→    println!("Ids:       {}", common::format_commit_short(commit));
    88→
    89→    // Author
    90→    let author = commit.author();
    91→    println!(
    92→        "Author:    {} <{}> ({})",
    93→        author.name,
    94→        author.email,
    95→        format_timestamp(&author.timestamp)
    96→    );
    97→
    98→    // Committer
    99→    let committer = commit.committer();
   100→    println!(
   101→        "Committer: {} <{}> ({})",
   102→        committer.name,
   103→        committer.email,
   104→        format_timestamp(&committer.timestamp)
   105→    );
   106→
   107→    // Parents
   108→    let root_commit_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
   109→    for parent_id in commit.parent_ids() {
   110→        if *parent_id == root_commit_id {
   111→            continue;
   112→        }
   113→        let parent = repo.store().get_commit(parent_id)?;
   114→        println!("Parent:    {}", common::format_commit_short(&parent));
   115→    }
   116→
   117→    // Children
   118→    let children_ids = common::resolve_revset(
   119→        workspace,
   120→        repo,
   121→        &format!("children({})", commit.id().hex()),
   122→    )?;
   123→    for child_id in &children_ids {
   124→        if *child_id == root_commit_id {
   125→            continue;
   126→        }
   127→        let child = repo.store().get_commit(child_id)?;
   128→        println!("Child:     {}", common::format_commit_short(&child));
   129→    }
   130→
   131→    // Branches (bookmarks whose ancestry includes this commit)
   132→    let branches_str = format_branches(commit, workspace, repo)?;
   133→    println!("Branches:  {branches_str}");
   134→
   135→    // Follows (nearest ancestor tag)
   136→    let follows = find_nearest_tag(commit, workspace, repo, true)?;
   137→    println!("Follows:   {}", follows.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));
   138→
   139→    // Precedes (nearest descendant tag)
   140→    let precedes = find_nearest_tag(commit, workspace, repo, false)?;
   141→    println!("Precedes:  {}", precedes.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));
   142→
   143→    Ok(())
   144→}
   145→
   146→fn find_nearest_tag(
   147→    commit: &Commit,
   148→    workspace: &Workspace,
   149→    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
   150→    ancestor: bool,
   151→) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   152→    let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();
   153→
   154→    // "follows": nearest ancestor tag = heads(tags() & ::commit)
   155→    // "precedes": nearest descendant tag = roots(tags() & commit::)
   156→    let revset_str = if ancestor {
   157→        format!("heads(tags() & ::{})", commit_hex)
   158→    } else {
   159→        format!("roots(tags() & {}::)", commit_hex)
   160→    };
   161→
   162→    let tag_ids = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;
   163→    if tag_ids.is_empty() {
   164→        return Ok(None);
   165→    }
   166→
   167→    // Find the tag name for the first result
   168→    let tag_id = &tag_ids[0];
   169→    let view = repo.view();
   170→    for (name, local_remote) in view.tags() {
   171→        if local_remote.local_target.added_ids().any(|id| id == tag_id) {
   172→            return Ok(Some(name.as_str().to_string()));
   173→        }
   174→    }
   175→
   176→    Ok(None)
   177→}
   178→
   179→fn format_branches(
   180→    commit: &Commit,
   181→    workspace: &Workspace,
   182→    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
   183→) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   184→    let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();
   185→    let view = repo.view();
   186→    let mut names: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   187→
   188→    for (name, local_remote) in view.bookmarks() {
   189→        // Check if commit is an ancestor of this local bookmark
   190→        for bookmark_id in local_remote.local_target.added_ids() {
   191→            let revset_str = format!(
   192→                "{} & ::{}",
   193→                commit_hex,
   194→                bookmark_id.hex()
   195→            );
   196→            let results = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;
   197→            if !results.is_empty() {
   198→                names.push(name.as_str().to_string());
   199→                break;
   200→            }
   201→        }
   202→
   203→        // Check remote bookmarks (skip "git" — internal colocated ref)
   204→        for (remote_name, remote_ref) in &local_remote.remote_refs {
   205→            if remote_name.as_str() == "git" {
   206→                continue;
   207→            }
   208→            for bookmark_id in remote_ref.target.added_ids() {
   209→                let revset_str = format!(
   210→                    "{} & ::{}",
   211→                    commit_hex,
   212→                    bookmark_id.hex()
   213→                );
   214→                let results = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;
   215→                if !results.is_empty() {
   216→                    names.push(format!("{}@{}", name.as_str(), remote_name.as_str()));
   217→                    break;
   218→                }
   219→            }
   220→        }
   221→    }
   222→
   223→    Ok(names.join(", "))
   224→}
   225→
   226→fn format_timestamp(ts: &jj_lib::backend::Timestamp) -> String {
   227→    let millis = ts.timestamp.0;
   228→    let secs = millis / 1000;
   229→    let tz_minutes = ts.tz_offset;
   230→
   231→    let dt = chrono::DateTime::from_timestamp(secs, 0)
   232→        .unwrap_or_default()
   233→        .with_timezone(
   234→            &chrono::FixedOffset::east_opt(tz_minutes * 60).unwrap_or(chrono::Utc.fix()),
   235→        );
   236→
   237→    dt.format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S").to_string()
   238→}
   239→
   240→fn print_description(commit: &Commit) {
   241→    let desc = commit.description();
   242→    // Skip the first line (title) — already shown on the Ids line
   243→    let body: String = desc
   244→        .lines()
   245→        .skip(1)
   246→        .skip_while(|l| l.is_empty()) // skip blank line between title and body
   247→        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
   248→        .join("\n");
   249→
   250→    if body.is_empty() {
   251→        println!("Description:");
   252→    } else {
   253→        println!("Description:");
   254→        for line in body.lines() {
   255→            println!("    {line}");
   256→        }
   257→    }
   258→    println!();
   259→}
   260→
   261→fn print_diff(
   262→    commit: &Commit,
   263→    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,
   264→    file_limit: usize,
   265→) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
   266→    let parent_tree = commit.parent_tree(repo.as_ref()).block_on()?;
   267→    let commit_tree = commit.tree();
   268→
   269→    let diff_iter = TreeDiffIterator::new(&parent_tree, &commit_tree, &EverythingMatcher);
   270→
   271→    let mut lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
   272→    for entry in diff_iter {
   273→        let diff = entry.values?;
   274→        let before = diff.before.as_resolved().cloned().flatten();
   275→        let after = diff.after.as_resolved().cloned().flatten();
   276→
   277→        // Skip tree (directory) entries — only show files
   278→        if matches!(&before, Some(TreeValue::Tree(_)))
   279→            || matches!(&after, Some(TreeValue::Tree(_)))
   280→        {
   281→            continue;
   282→        }
   283→
   284→        let path_str = entry.path.as_internal_file_string().to_string();
   285→
   286→        let line = match (before, after) {
   287→            (None, Some(value)) => {
   288→                format!("    Added {} {path_str}", file_type_str(&value))
   289→            }
   290→            (Some(_), None) => {
   291→                format!(
   292→                    "    Removed {} {path_str}",
   293→                    file_type_str_from_merged(&diff.before),
   294→                )
   295→            }
   296→            (Some(_), Some(value)) => {
   297→                format!("    Modified {} {path_str}", file_type_str(&value))
   298→            }
   299→            (None, None) => continue,
   300→        };
   301→        lines.push(line);
   302→    }
   303→
   304→    let total = lines.len();
   305→    let show_all = file_limit == 0 || total <= file_limit;
   306→
   307→    if show_all {
   308→        println!("Changed files: {total}");
   309→        for line in &lines {
   310→            println!("{line}");
   311→        }
   312→    } else {
   313→        let half = file_limit / 2;
   314→        let tail_start = total.saturating_sub(half);
   315→        let skipped = total - half - half;
   316→        println!("Changed files: {total} (showing {file_limit})");
   317→        for line in &lines[..half] {
   318→            println!("{line}");
   319→        }
   320→        println!("    ... {skipped} more ...");
   321→        for line in &lines[tail_start..] {
   322→            println!("{line}");
   323→        }
   324→    }
   325→
   326→    Ok(())
   327→}
   328→
   329→fn file_type_str(value: &TreeValue) -> &'static str {
   330→    match value {
   331→        TreeValue::File {
   332→            executable: false, ..
   333→        } => "regular file",
   334→        TreeValue::File {
   335→            executable: true, ..
   336→        } => "executable file",
   337→        TreeValue::Symlink(_) => "symlink",
   338→        TreeValue::Tree(_) => "tree",
   339→        TreeValue::GitSubmodule(_) => "git submodule",
   340→    }
   341→}
   342→
   343→fn file_type_str_from_merged(merged: &jj_lib::merge::Merge<Option<TreeValue>>) -> &'static str {
   344→    if let Some(Some(value)) = merged.as_resolved() {
   345→        file_type_str(value)
   346→    } else {
   347→        "file"
   348→    }
   349→}
   350→

repo)?;\n    println!(\"Branches:  {branches_str}\");\n\n    // Follows\n    let follows = find_nearest_tag(commit, workspace, repo, true)?;\n    println!(\"Follows:   {}\", follows.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\"));\n\n    // Precedes\n    let precedes = find_nearest_tag(commit, workspace, repo, false)?;\n    println!(\"Precedes:  {}\", precedes.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\"));\n\n    // Description (body only — title is on the Ids line)\n    print_description(commit);\n\n    // Changed files\n    if file_limit != FileLimit::None {\n        print_diff(commit, repo, file_limit)?;\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn find_nearest_tag(\n    commit: &Commit,\n    workspace: &Workspace,\n    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n    ancestor: bool,\n) -> Result<Option<String>, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();\n\n    let revset_str = if ancestor {\n        format!(\"heads(tags() & ::{})\", commit_hex)\n    } else {\n        format!(\"roots(tags() & {}::)\", commit_hex)\n    };\n\n    let tag_ids = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;\n    if tag_ids.is_empty() {\n        return Ok(None);\n    }\n\n    let tag_id = &tag_ids[0];\n    let view = repo.view();\n    for (name, local_remote) in view.tags() {\n        if local_remote.local_target.added_ids().any(|id| id == tag_id) {\n            return Ok(Some(name.as_str().to_string()));\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(None)\n}\n\nfn format_branches(\n    commit: &Commit,\n    workspace: &Workspace,\n    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n) -> Result<String, Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let commit_hex = commit.id().hex();\n    let view = repo.view();\n    let mut names: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n\n    for (name, local_remote) in view.bookmarks() {\n        for bookmark_id in local_remote.local_target.added_ids() {\n            let revset_str = format!(\"{} & ::{}\", commit_hex, bookmark_id.hex());\n            let results = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;\n            if !results.is_empty() {\n                names.push(name.as_str().to_string());\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (remote_name, remote_ref) in &local_remote.remote_refs {\n            if remote_name.as_str() == \"git\" {\n                continue;\n            }\n            for bookmark_id in remote_ref.target.added_ids() {\n                let revset_str = format!(\"{} & ::{}\", commit_hex, bookmark_id.hex());\n                let results = common::resolve_revset(workspace, repo, &revset_str)?;\n                if !results.is_empty() {\n                    names.push(format!(\"{}@{}\", name.as_str(), remote_name.as_str()));\n                    break;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(names.join(\", \"))\n}\n\nfn format_timestamp(ts: &jj_lib::backend::Timestamp) -> String {\n    let millis = ts.timestamp.0;\n    let secs = millis / 1000;\n    let tz_minutes = ts.tz_offset;\n\n    let dt = chrono::DateTime::from_timestamp(secs, 0)\n        .unwrap_or_default()\n        .with_timezone(\n            &chrono::FixedOffset::east_opt(tz_minutes * 60).unwrap_or(chrono::Utc.fix()),\n        );\n\n    dt.format(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\").to_string()\n}\n\nfn print_description(commit: &Commit) {\n    let desc = commit.description();\n    let body: String = desc\n        .lines()\n        .skip(1)\n        .skip_while(|l| l.is_empty())\n        .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n        .join(\"\\n\");\n\n    if body.is_empty() {\n        println!(\"Description:\");\n    } else {\n        println!(\"Description:\");\n        for line in body.lines() {\n            println!(\"    {line}\");\n        }\n    }\n    println!();\n}\n\nfn print_diff(\n    commit: &Commit,\n    repo: &Arc<ReadonlyRepo>,\n    file_limit: FileLimit,\n) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n    let parent_tree = commit.parent_tree(repo.as_ref()).block_on()?;\n    let commit_tree = commit.tree();\n\n    let diff_iter = TreeDiffIterator::new(&parent_tree, &commit_tree, &EverythingMatcher);\n\n    let mut lines: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n    for entry in diff_iter {\n        let diff = entry.values?;\n        let before = diff.before.as_resolved().cloned().flatten();\n        let after = diff.after.as_resolved().cloned().flatten();\n\n        if matches!(&before, Some(TreeValue::Tree(_)))\n            || matches!(&after, Some(TreeValue::Tree(_)))\n        {\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        let path_str = entry.path.as_internal_file_string().to_string();\n\n        let line = match (before, after) {\n            (None, Some(value)) => {\n                format!(\"    Added {} {path_str}\", file_type_str(&value))\n            }\n            (Some(_), None) => {\n                format!(\n                    \"    Removed {} {path_str}\",\n                    file_type_str_from_merged(&diff.before),\n                )\n            }\n            (Some(_), Some(value)) => {\n                format!(\"    Modified {} {path_str}\", file_type_str(&value))\n            }\n            (None, None) => continue,\n        };\n        lines.push(line);\n    }\n\n    let total = lines.len();\n    let cap = match file_limit {\n        FileLimit::All | FileLimit::None => 0,\n        FileLimit::Cap(n) => n,\n    };\n    let show_all = cap == 0 || total <= cap;\n\n    if show_all {\n        println!(\"Changed files: {total}\");\n        for line in &lines {\n            println!(\"{line}\");\n        }\n    } else {\n        let half = cap / 2;\n        let tail_start = total.saturating_sub(half);\n        let skipped = total - half - half;\n        println!(\"Changed files: {total} (showing {cap})\");\n        for line in &lines[..half] {\n            println!(\"{line}\");\n        }\n        println!(\"    ... {skipped} more ...\");\n        for line in &lines[tail_start..] {\n            println!(\"{line}\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\nfn file_type_str(value: &TreeValue) -> &'static str {\n    match value {\n        TreeValue::File {\n            executable: false, ..\n        } => \"regular file\",\n        TreeValue::File {\n            executable: true, ..\n        } => \"executable file\",\n        TreeValue::Symlink(_) => \"symlink\",\n        TreeValue::Tree(_) => \"tree\",\n        TreeValue::GitSubmodule(_) => \"git submodule\",\n    }\n}\n\nfn file_type_str_from_merged(merged: &jj_lib::merge::Merge<Option<TreeValue>>) -> &'static str {\n    if let Some(Some(value)) = merged.as_resolved() {\n        file_type_str(value)\n    } else {\n        \"file\"\n    }\n}\n"
}

    bookmarks (local + remote), author/committer with timestamps,
    indented description, and file-level diff summary (Added/Modified/Removed).
    Uses TreeDiffIterator for sync tree diffing via jj-lib.
    
    ochid: /.claude/qpyuvvkkpluy
```

    10→    /// Revision (with optional .. notation)
    11→    #[arg()]
    12→    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,
    13→
    14→    /// Count (items per dotted side)
    15→    #[arg()]
    16→    pub pos_count: Option<usize>,
    17→
    18→    /// Revision to describe (default: @)
    19→    #[arg(short, long, default_value = "@")]
    20→    pub revision: String,
    21→
    22→    /// Path to jj repo (default: current directory)
    23→    #[arg(short = 'R', long, default_value = ".")]
    24→    pub repo: PathBuf,
    25→
    26→    /// Maximum number of commits to describe
    27→    #[arg(short, long)]

    31→    #[arg(short, long, default_value = "3")]
    32→    pub indent: usize,
    33→}
    34→
    35→pub fn desc(args: &DescArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    36→    let resolved = common::resolve_dot_args(
    37→        args.pos_rev.as_deref(),
    38→        args.pos_count,
    39→        &args.revision,
    40→        args.limit,
    41→        "@",
    42→    );
    43→
    44→    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&args.repo)?;
    45→
    46→    let commit_ids = common::resolve_revset(&workspace, &repo, &resolved.revset)?;
    47→
    48→    if commit_ids.is_empty() {
    49→        return Err(format!("no commit found for revision '{}'", resolved.revset).into());
    50→    }
    51→
    52→    let root_commit_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
    53→
    54→    if let Some(both_count) = resolved.both_count {
    55→        let (lines, anchor) = common::collect_both(
    56→            &repo,
    57→            &args.repo,
    58→            args.pos_rev.as_deref(),
    59→            both_count,
    60→            common::format_commit_full,
    61→        )?;
    62→        for (i, line) in lines.iter().enumerate() {
    63→            let indented = common::indent_body(line, args.indent);
    64→            if i == anchor {
    65→                println!("{}", common::bold_first_line(&indented));
    66→            } else {
    67→                println!("{indented}");
    68→            }
    69→        }
    70→        return Ok(());
    71→    }
    72→
    73→    // Resolve the primary commit ID for highlighting
    74→    let primary_ids = common::resolve_revset(&workspace, &repo, &resolved.primary_rev)?;
    75→    let primary_id = primary_ids.first();
    76→
    77→    let mut results: Vec<(String, bool)> = Vec::new();
    78→    let mut count = 0;
    79→    for commit_id in &commit_ids {
    80→        if *commit_id == root_commit_id {
    81→            continue;
    82→        }
    83→        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
    84→        let is_primary = primary_id == Some(commit_id);
    85→        results.push((common::format_commit_full(&commit), is_primary));
    86→
    87→        count += 1;
    88→        if let Some(limit) = resolved.limit
    89→            && count >= limit
    90→        {
    91→            break;
    92→        }
    93→    }
    94→
    95→    for (line, is_primary) in &results {
    96→        let indented = common::indent_body(line, args.indent);
    97→        if *is_primary {
    98→            println!("{}", common::bold_first_line(&indented));
    99→        } else {
   100→            println!("{indented}");
   101→        }
   102→    }
   103→
   104→    Ok(())
   105→}

> AGENT

Wait — `indent_body` indents all lines *after* the first. But here every line needs indenting. So `indent_body` wouldn't work directly unless we prepend a fake first line.

Actually, the cleaner fix is to make a small `indent_all` helper in common, or just keep the loop. The loop is 2 lines — adding a helper for it is over-engineering. Let me leave it.

> DEVELOPER

The first line it "always" empty, isn't it when using two -m in `jj describe` ?

> AGENT

Ah — you mean the raw description from a `jj commit -m "title" -m "body"` has:

```
title
\n
body line 1
body line 2
```

So after `skip(1).skip_while(empty)`, the body's "first line" is `body line 1`, and `indent_body` would leave that unindented while indenting the rest. That's wrong — we want all body lines indented equally.

`indent_body` is designed for `desc`/`list` output where the first line is `chid commitid title` and shouldn't be indented. Different use case.

So no, `indent_body` doesn't fit here. The manual loop is correct.

> DEVELOPER

Weird, in desc indent_body is used two places format_commit_full is invoked one place and format_commit_full is passed to collect_both, seems overly complicated and we could make it "simpler", your thoughts, don't change anything

> AGENT

You're right it's convoluted. The chain is:

1. `format_commit_full` returns a multi-line string: `chid commitid title\nbody_line1\nbody_line2`
2. `indent_body` indents everything after the first line (so the body gets indented but the chid/commitid line doesn't)
3. `collect_both` takes a formatter function, returns pre-formatted strings

The complexity comes from `format_commit_full` combining the IDs and the full description into one string, then `indent_body` treating the first line specially. It works but it's a leaky abstraction — the formatting and the indenting are tightly coupled but live in different places.

A simpler model: have the commit formatting and display be one step, not two. Like `show_one_commit` in `show.rs` — it just prints directly, no intermediate multi-line string that needs special re-indenting.

If we ever refactor `desc` to use the same `show_one_commit` pattern (or a shared `print_commit` that takes options for what to include), the `format_commit_full` + `indent_body` dance goes away. But that's a bigger refactor — not worth it right now.

> DEVELOPER

Ok finalize steps for v0.18.1 and we'll do the cleanup in v0.18.2.

> TOOL
